INDEX IN ZONARÆ ANNALES.
INDEX OF MEMORABLE MATTERS CONTAINED IN ZONARAS' ANNALS.
The letter b denotes [references to] the volume of the Royal edition, which in this most recent [edition] begins at col. 1097 of the previous volume. We have expressed the pages of the same Royal edition in larger type in the Latin text.
